{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
}{{{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@transformcm{1.0}{0.0}{0.0}{1.0}{-7.8941pt}{-22.76228pt}\lxSVG@begingroup@{transform} \pgfsys@hbox{60}\lxSVG@closescope }}}
\lxSVG@closescope }}}
\lxSVG@closescope {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}=\frac{9}{u_{0}}\,\frac{2^{3+\epsilon}}{S_{d}(d-2)}\,z^{\prime 1+\epsilon}\,\zeta^{3}\,\sum_{i=1}^{4}g_{i}(\zeta)\,,

(151)

where

\displaystyle g_{1}(\zeta)

\displaystyle=-\frac{(2+\epsilon)\,\zeta^{-4}}{12(-1+\epsilon)_{6}}-\frac{\zeta^{-2}}{72(1-\epsilon^{2})(2+\epsilon)}+\frac{2-\epsilon}{120\,\epsilon^{2}(1-\epsilon)(5-\epsilon)}-\frac{(24-\epsilon^{2})\,\zeta^{\epsilon}}{180\,\epsilon^{2}(2+\epsilon)(4+\epsilon)(5+\epsilon)},

\displaystyle g_{2}(\zeta)

\displaystyle=\frac{3+\epsilon}{12(-1+\epsilon)_{7}}\,\left(\zeta^{-5}f_{3}(\zeta)+f_{4}(\zeta)\right)\,,

(152)

\displaystyle g_{3}(\zeta)

\displaystyle=-\frac{(7+3\epsilon)\epsilon}{24(-1+\epsilon)_{7}}\,\left(\zeta^{-3}\,f_{3}(\zeta)+\zeta^{-2}f_{4}(\zeta)\right)\,,

\displaystyle g_{4}(\zeta)

\displaystyle=\frac{10+\epsilon-\epsilon^{2}}{24\,(-1+\epsilon)_{7}}\,\left(\zeta^{-1}f_{3}(\zeta)+\zeta^{-4}f_{4}(\zeta)\right)\,,

f_{3}(\zeta)=(\zeta+1)^{\epsilon}-(1-\zeta)^{\epsilon}\,,\qquad\qquad f_{4}(\zeta)=(\zeta+1)^{\epsilon}+(1-\zeta)^{\epsilon}\,,

(153)

and
(a)_{k}=a(a+1)\ldots(a+k-1)
are again the Pochhammer symbols.

(154)

where we defined

\displaystyle B^{LT}(y,y^{\prime})=\int d^{d-1}r\,G^{L}_{0}(r;y,y^{\prime})\,G^{T}_{0}(r;y,y^{\prime})=\frac{2^{\epsilon}}{S_{d}(d-2)}\,b^{LT}(y,y^{\prime})\,.

(155)

A straightforward calculation yields

\displaystyle b^{LT}(y,y^{\prime}|y<y^{\prime})=\frac{|y_{-}|^{-1+\epsilon}-y_{+}^{-1+\epsilon}}{4(1-\epsilon)}-\frac{2}{\epsilon\,(1+\epsilon)\,yy^{\prime}}\left(|y_{-}|^{1+\epsilon}+y_{+}^{1+\epsilon}-\frac{4-\epsilon}{2+\epsilon}\,y^{\prime 1+\epsilon}\right)+

\displaystyle 6\,\frac{y_{+}^{3+\epsilon}-|y_{-}|^{3+\epsilon}}{\epsilon\,(2+\epsilon)\,(3+\epsilon)y^{2}y^{\prime 2}}-\frac{6}{\epsilon\,(2+\epsilon)(3+\epsilon)(5+\epsilon)}\left(\frac{|y_{-}|^{5+\epsilon}+y_{+}^{5+\epsilon}}{y^{3}y^{\prime 3}}+2\,\frac{|y_{-}|^{2}-(3+\epsilon)|y_{-}|y_{+}+y_{+}^{2}}{(1+\epsilon)\,y^{3}}\;y^{\prime\epsilon}\right).

The double integral in (
154
) can be done by analogy to the one in (
145
).
The first integration yields

\displaystyle\hskip-4.0pt\hbox to92.03pt{\vbox to58.29pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 46.01215pt\lower-28.59529pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin}
{{}}\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\hbox{\hbox{{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {{}{}{{
{}{}}}{
{}{}}
{{}{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}}{{}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}{}{{}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
}{{{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@transformcm{1.0}{0.0}{0.0}{1.0}{-42.67914pt}{0.0pt}\lxSVG@begingroup@{transform} \pgfsys@hbox{60}\lxSVG@closescope }}}

(156)

with

\displaystyle\tilde{Y}_{d_{T}}(\zeta)

\displaystyle=\int_{1}^{\infty}d{\cal{Z}}\,b^{LT}(1,{\cal{Z}})\,{\cal{Z}}^{-2}\,f_{T}\Big(\frac{\zeta}{\cal Z}\Big)+\int_{1}^{1/\zeta}d{\cal{Z}}\,b^{LT}(1,{\cal{Z}})\,{\cal{Z}}^{1-\epsilon}\,f_{T}\big(\zeta{\cal Z}\big)

\displaystyle+\zeta^{-3+\epsilon}\int_{1/\zeta}^{\infty}d{\cal{Z}}\,b^{LT}(1,{\cal{Z}})\,{\cal{Z}}^{-2}\,f_{T}\left((\zeta{\cal Z})^{-1}\right).

(157)

{\cal{Z}}
in (
B
) gives the result

}{{{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@transformcm{1.0}{0.0}{0.0}{1.0}{-9.64116pt}{-22.76228pt}\lxSVG@begingroup@{transform} \pgfsys@hbox{60}\lxSVG@closescope }}}
\lxSVG@closescope }}}
\lxSVG@closescope {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}=\frac{9}{u_{0}}\,\frac{2^{2+\epsilon}}{S_{d}(d-2)}\,z^{\prime 1+\epsilon}\,\zeta^{2}\,\sum_{i=1}^{4}q_{i}(\zeta)\,,

(158)

with

\displaystyle q_{1}(\zeta)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{(\epsilon)_{6}}\,\zeta^{-4}-\frac{3-\epsilon}{3(-1+\epsilon)_{5}}\,\zeta^{-2}+\frac{1+(3-\epsilon)\epsilon}{9\epsilon^{2}(1-\epsilon^{2})(2+\epsilon)(3-\epsilon)}-\frac{2(2-\epsilon)}{9\epsilon^{2}(2+\epsilon)(3+\epsilon)(4+\epsilon)}\zeta^{\epsilon}\,,

\displaystyle q_{2}(\zeta)

\displaystyle=\frac{50-21\epsilon+\epsilon^{2}}{12(-1+\epsilon)_{7}}\,\left(\zeta^{-3}f_{3}(\zeta)+f_{4}(\zeta)\right)\,,

(159)

\displaystyle q_{3}(\zeta)

\displaystyle=\frac{40-11\epsilon+\epsilon^{2}}{4(-1+\epsilon)_{7}}\,\left(\zeta^{-1}f_{3}(\zeta)+\zeta^{-2}f_{4}(\zeta)\right)\,,

\displaystyle q_{4}(\zeta)

\displaystyle=\frac{1-\epsilon}{2(-1+\epsilon)_{7}}\,\left(\zeta f_{3}(\zeta)+\zeta^{-4}f_{4}(\zeta)\right)\,,

where
f_{3}(\zeta)
and
f_{4}(\zeta)
are defined in (
153
).
After some algebra, the results (
151
) and (
158
) can be brought to a form
similar to (
140
).
